- **DECISIONS**: &gt; **Updated:** 2026-11-24 | **Related:** [GEMINI](../../GEMINI.md) | [STATE](STATE.md) | [PROJECT_MAP](PROJECT_MAP.md) | [VALIDATION](VALIDATION.md) ([ThanhSi1008/ai-video-upscaler](https://github.com/ThanhSi1008/ai-video-upscaler/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/DECISIONS.md))
- **DECISIONS**: Append-only. Numbered. Dated. Everything else references the number. ([zerwone/staked](https://github.com/zerwone/staked/blob/HEAD/agents/DECISIONS.md))
- **DECISIONS**: Owner decisions only. Append-only, numbered, dated. Everything else cites the number (D-001). Hand-written by the owner; staked never generates or edits this file. ([zerwone/staked](https://github.com/zerwone/staked/blob/HEAD/examples/two-agents/agents/DECISIONS.md))
- **decisions-agent**: Specialized agent for updating `decisions-index.md`. Clean context. Work only on today's chronicle and the index file. ([enzolona/claude-project-memory](https://github.com/enzolona/claude-project-memory/blob/HEAD/skills/daily-project-chronicle/agents/decisions-agent.md) · ★1)
- **decisions-breadcrumb**: During implementation, the agent drops **breadcrumbs** — small structured decision notes — into `.scratch/&lt;feature&gt;/decisions.md` in this repo. `prepare-for-review` reads them at PR time and surfaces each one either in the PR body (as a bullet under "Alternatives considered") or as an inline review comment anchored to a specific line. ([opengovsg/FormSG](https://github.com/opengovsg/FormSG/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/decisions-breadcrumb.md) · ★375)
- **decisions-correlator**: Given a scope prose + mode, globs docs/decisions/*.md, reads only the frontmatter of each candidate, applies a per-mode candidate-pool filter, and returns a ranked shortlist of decisions docs semantically correlated with the scope. Used by plan-context in both phase mode (pool = ad-hoc docs with `related_phases: []`) and task mode (pool = all docs except the task's own) to surface potentially relevant prior decisions without loading their bodies. The main thread asks the user to confirm the final set via AskUserQuestion. ([devfullcycle/mba-ia-greenfield-project](https://github.com/devfullcycle/mba-ia-greenfield-project/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/decisions-correlator.md) · ★28 · 14 copies)
- **decisions-detail-reader**: Reads the decisions documents tied to a target phase or task and extracts, per decided TD, only the `**Recommendation:**` prose + `**Libraries:**` line. Returns a compact `## Decisions Detail for Phase NN` / `## Decisions Detail for Task {slug}` block ready for direct embedding in context.md. Use when a stage of the plan pipeline needs the per-TD prose that `/plan-build` consumes when drafting Technical actions, without loading full TD bodies into the main thread. ([devfullcycle/mba-ia-greenfield-project](https://github.com/devfullcycle/mba-ia-greenfield-project/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/decisions-detail-reader.md) · ★28 · 14 copies)
- **decisions-format**: **Rule (owner, 2026-10-09):** if a PR or ticket needs the owner to choose something, it gives him the options to answer, and it can't be approved until the required ones are answered. Asking in prose ("A, B or C?", a "Choose" or "Open questions" heading, a checklist item ending in "?") is **too vague**: the dashboard shows *Too vague: needs options*, hides Approve, and the merge server refuses it ([G-Eskayo/marvin](https://github.com/G-Eskayo/marvin/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/decisions-format.md) · ★3)
- **decisions-generator**: Phase-2 macro-agent. Thin wrapper that preloads the `decisions-generator` skill and runs it in an isolated context with tool whitelisting. Generates stress-tested ADRs via divergent candidates + adversarial pairwise comparison. Use proactively when Phase-1 artefacts exist and ADRs must be drafted, stress-tested, or accepted — triggers: "generate decisions", "propose ADRs", "run Phase 2", "evaluate options", "accept the flow-variant decision", "stress-test this decision". ([rd162/OSS_Migration](https://github.com/rd162/OSS_Migration/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/decisions-generator.md))
- **decisions-hunter**: Analyze the provided conversation log and extract **technical decisions**. ([Alex-Kopylov/zweihander](https://github.com/Alex-Kopylov/zweihander/blob/HEAD/plugins/ai-assistant-ops/skills/ai-insights-hunter/agents/decisions-hunter.md) · ★8)

Used as input to the handoff synthesis process. ([driver-ai/driver-sdlc-plugin](https://github.com/driver-ai/driver-sdlc-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/decisions-log.md) · ★3)
- **decisions-reader**: Reads and filters technical decisions documents tied to a target phase or task. Returns a compact, structured index of all TDs (phase mode: phase-scope doc + ad-hoc docs with the phase in related_phases; task mode: the task's own ad-hoc doc if present) with their status, scope, topic, decision (if decided), and libraries (if any). Use when a stage of the plan pipeline needs the decisions landscape without loading the raw docs into the main thread. ([devfullcycle/mba-ia-greenfield-project](https://github.com/devfullcycle/mba-ia-greenfield-project/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/decisions-reader.md) · ★28 · 14 copies)

- **decode**: 你是受协调者委派的方向 agent。只处理提供的 experiment_id、baseline_id 与不可变 manifest。 开始：核对实际仓库、worktree、完整 commit、有效配置、硬件/软件、数据与初始 checkpoint；先查当前已启用优化和负结果库。 权限：只改契约指定文件；禁止修改基线、计时器、验收阈值、采样规则和检查器来获得通过。Git 提交由有权限的协调执行端完成。 调研：优先官方文档、原论文与当前实现；记录版本及硬件前提，不把别人的速度作为当前负载收益。 实验：一次说明一个可证伪假设；多个因素的组合明确标注，先小测，再真实组件，最后独占完整验收。 资源：提交作业只通过指定调度接口；同一幂等键重试先查 job_id；不得自行抢 GPU、杀他人进程或重复重启 rank。 证据：真实命令、退出码、源样本/增强/权重指纹、raw trace/metrics、完整时 ([626619403/Ergora](https://github.com/626619403/Ergora/blob/HEAD/src/ergora/data/reference/platform/upstream/agents/decode.md) · ★2)
- **decode-referee**: Independently re-derives one Design Card's measurement from the source it cites, blind to the reasoning that produced it, so a card nobody can reproduce is dropped before it becomes a token. One card per dispatch, and it receives only the cited source location and the claim — never the authoring agent's rationale, confidence or summary. Read-only: it re-measures and reports agreement or disagreement, and never edits a card or writes a file. Started by the deep-audit skill. ([Braydenh563/Tessellate](https://github.com/Braydenh563/Tessellate/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/deep-audit-ux.md) · ★4)
- **deep-auditor**: Composite health audit that runs test-health, config-drift-detect, hook-effectiveness, security-audit, mcp-audit, plugin-audit, and socket-audit in parallel, reconciles into severity-ranked findings, cross-checks against prior decisions via RAG, and produces a prioritized remediation plan. Use for "is this project healthy", before releases, or weekly per active repo. ([LucasSantana-Dev/sharekit-profile](https://github.com/LucasSantana-Dev/sharekit-profile/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/deep-auditor.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **deep-auditor**: Use when a pull request or changeset needs a deep audit - a combined security audit, performance audit, and test audit in one pass. For each finding it builds a proof (exploit, benchmark, or failing case), verifies a fix against that proof, and returns findings, proofs, and verified fixes for the main agent to apply. Use proactively after a PR is done and before merge, or when asked for a thorough/full review. ([ifiokjr/dotfiles](https://github.com/ifiokjr/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/Configs/agents/.zcode/agents/deep-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **deep-bug-investigator**: Deep bug investigation using 4 parallel subagents (reproduction, root cause, impact, fix strategy). Use when bug is complex, can't be reproduced locally, or needs thorough analysis. Spawns fresh-context subagents for each investigation track. ([oliver-kriska/claude-elixir-phoenix](https://github.com/oliver-kriska/claude-elixir-phoenix/blob/HEAD/plugins/elixir-phoenix/agents/deep-bug-investigator.md) · ★576 · 7 copies)
- **deep-bug-investigator**: Investigates tricky Ruby/Rails/Grape bugs, race conditions, stale cache problems, and Sidekiq failures using structured evidence gathering. Writes the investigation report to a file. ([slbug/claude-ruby-grape-rails](https://github.com/slbug/claude-ruby-grape-rails/blob/HEAD/plugins/ruby-grape-rails/agents/deep-bug-investigator.md) · ★7 · 2 copies)
- **deep-bug-investigator**: Root-cause analysis for complex .NET bugs, crashes, hangs, memory leaks. Spawns 4 parallel subagents (reproduction, root cause, impact, fix strategy). Use when a bug resists simple fixes or recurs. ([dimsour/dotnet-ai-toolkit](https://github.com/dimsour/dotnet-ai-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/dotnet/agents/deep-bug-investigator.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **deep-bug-investigator**: Coordinate 4 parallel investigation tracks to diagnose complex React bugs. Spawned by /rx:investigate for bugs spanning state, rendering, network, and environment. ([surfertas/claude-react-rtk](https://github.com/surfertas/claude-react-rtk/blob/HEAD/plugins/react-rtk/agents/deep-bug-investigator.md))
- **deep-bug-scan**: Deep scan a folder for bugs — logic errors, race conditions, SQL issues, assertion gaps. ([cbmono/ai-setup](https://github.com/cbmono/ai-setup/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/deep-bug-scan.md) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **deep-bug-scanner**: Use this agent for deep bug detection that traces full call graphs of changed functions. Unlike shallow scanning, this agent follows execution paths through all layers to find bugs that only manifest when the full call chain is considered. &lt;example&gt; Context: A PR changes a validation function used by multiple API endpoints. user: "Review PR #42 for bugs" assistant: "I'll use the deep-bug-scanner to trace the full call graph of the changed validation function." &lt;commentary&gt; The validation change could affect multiple callers - deep scanning traces all paths. &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt;… ([bradwindy/ultimate-code-review](https://github.com/bradwindy/ultimate-code-review/blob/HEAD/agents/deep-bug-scanner.md) · ★4)
- **deep-build**: Autonomous deep worker - explores thoroughly, executes end-to-end, asks only as last resort ([dheerkt/dotfiles](https://github.com/dheerkt/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/opencode/agents/deep-build.md) · 2 copies)
- **deep-check**: Master orchestrator agent. Runs all 14 screenplay analysis agents in optimal sequence (foundation → macro → details → synthesis). Consolidates reports into single dashboard. Returns comprehensive screenplay audit. ([aslanSuleimenov/claude-screenplay-plugin](https://github.com/aslanSuleimenov/claude-screenplay-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/deep-check.md))
- **deep-checkup**: Full health check of FoolProof and every system around it — the running bot, the server, CI, the hooks, the documents, the Claude Code memory and rules. Manual and rare, roughly weekly. Trusts nothing but executed behaviour, writes evidence for every claim, fixes nothing. ([oleg-vasilyev/FoolProof](https://github.com/oleg-vasilyev/FoolProof/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/deep-checkup.md) · ★2)
- **deep-code-auditor**: Use for deep, read-only code and diff audits, bug-fix validation, regression checks, and correctness investigations. Trace related execution paths and report evidence-backed defects, risks, and coverage limits without modifying files. ([garbageek/claude-plugins](https://github.com/garbageek/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/agents/deep-code-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **deep-code-reasoning**: Context-aware vulnerability detection beyond static patterns. RECON-scoped freeform reasoning about IDOR, confused deputy, TOCTOU, privilege escalation, workflow bypass. Phase 1b peer agent. ([bdfinst/agentic-dev-team](https://github.com/bdfinst/agentic-dev-team/blob/HEAD/plugins/security-assessment/agents/deep-code-reasoning.md) · ★309)
- **deep-code-reasoning**: Static analysis catches known patterns; this agent catches context- dependent vulnerabilities that require understanding how components interact across the codebase — issues that only appear when reading the code the way a human security researcher would: tracing data flows, following call chains, and reasoning about authorization design intent vs. implementation. ([bdfinst/agentic-dev-team](https://github.com/bdfinst/agentic-dev-team/blob/HEAD/plugins/security-assessment/docs/agents/deep-code-reasoning.md) · ★309)
- **deep-code-reviewer**: Thorough 6-aspect code review covering correctness, security, performance, maintainability, verification, and documentation. Use for comprehensive PR reviews or code quality audits. ([travisjneuman/.claude](https://github.com/travisjneuman/.claude/blob/HEAD/agents/deep-code-reviewer.md) · ★113)
- **deep-code-reviewer**: Deep code quality review — correctness, security, performance for critical reviews ([egerev/superflow](https://github.com/egerev/superflow/blob/HEAD/agents/deep-code-reviewer.md) · ★40)
- **deep-context-analyzer**: Use this agent for exhaustive, file-level code analysis. It cross-references code with project documentation, maps business logic and risks, and returns structured findings without modifying code. ([nico0695/cors-bridge](https://github.com/nico0695/cors-bridge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/deep-context-analyzer.agent.md))
- **deep-context-gatherer**: Multi-phase research agent combining parallel web searches with local codebase analysis ([otrebu/agents](https://github.com/otrebu/agents/blob/HEAD/plugins/development-lifecycle/agents/deep-context-gatherer.md) · ★4)
- **deep-cr-finder**: Read-only worker agent for the deep-cr Workflow (Tier 2 code review). Driven by the workflow's per-call prompts, it plays one of three roles — a lens finder that runs git diff and reports candidate findings, a scorer that independently re-verifies one finding's cited contract and file:line, or the synthesis pass that classifies survivors. Do not invoke directly for ad-hoc review; for a single-pass review use vesicle-cr-reviewer instead. ([3aKHP/prism-vesicle](https://github.com/3aKHP/prism-vesicle/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/deep-cr-finder.md))
- **deep-crawler**: Executes multi-modal web search and stealth Playwright crawls across arXiv, OpenAlex, GitHub, YouTube, HackerNews, and Wikipedia ([kenhuangus/packt-harness](https://github.com/kenhuangus/packt-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/deep-crawler.md) · ★13 · 2 copies)
- **deep-critic**: Thợ săn điểm mù chuyên nghiệp. Nhận một vấn đề + một lăng kính phản biện được giao + danh sách phát hiện đã có, và trả về CHỈ những điểm yếu/điểm mù MỚI chưa ai tìm ra. Dùng agent này khi skill deep-critique cần chạy một vòng phản biện độc lập, hoặc bất cứ khi nào cần một góc nhìn phản biện tách biệt khỏi hội thoại chính. ([elisehanhnguyenus-cloud/purrlight-skills](https://github.com/elisehanhnguyenus-cloud/purrlight-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/deep-critique/agents/deep-critic.md))
- **deep-cuts**: Structural deep-cuts analyser for any cianfhoghlaim subdirectory. Given a path (e.g. 'dlt/', 'baml_src/', 'cocoindex/'), spawn parallel deep-cuts subagents to map files, schemas, patterns, design choices, and identified drift; then synthesise a unified report with 20 questions per subdirectory for human deep-dive. Hidden (programmatic-only invocation). Uses Gemma 4 26B-A4B via Unsloth Studio. ([cianfhoghlaim/cianfhoghlaim](https://github.com/cianfhoghlaim/cianfhoghlaim/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/deep-cuts.md) · ★4)
- **deep-cuts**: Structural deep-cuts analyser for any cianfhoghlaim subdirectory. Given a path (e.g. 'dlt/', 'baml_src/', 'cocoindex/'), spawn parallel deep-cuts subagents to map files, schemas, patterns, design choices, and identified drift; then synthesise a unified report with 20 questions per subdirectory for human deep-dive. Hidden (programmatic-only invocation). ([cianfhoghlaim/ciandlithe](https://github.com/cianfhoghlaim/ciandlithe/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/deep-cuts.md) · ★1)
- **deep-debug**: Isolated deep-reasoning agent for a defect whose cause is genuinely unknown after a first look — a gargoyle that goes silent on one client only, an RPC that appears to do nothing, a state machine that sticks, a desync between host and client, a change that works in the editor and not in game. Give it the symptom and the evidence gathered so far; it returns a diagnosis with the proof. Not for defects already understood, and not for implementing a known fix. ([DropDaDeuce/LethalGargoyles](https://github.com/DropDaDeuce/LethalGargoyles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/deep-debug.md) · ★1)
- **deep-debug**: Root-cause investigation for bugs whose cause is genuinely unknown — intermittent failures, heisenbugs, "it works locally", corruption that appears far from its source, performance cliffs. Use only after cheaper approaches have failed to identify the cause. ([Bijaykars/claude-code-autoroute](https://github.com/Bijaykars/claude-code-autoroute/blob/HEAD/agents/deep-debug.md))
